## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单严格 `cuda:0,1,2,3` on `rtx5090-4x`，无其他服务器可用
- 所有 eval 命令**必须带 `--sft_adapter` + `--no-quantize`**（D064 教训：缺 `--sft_adapter` 导致 rel_f1=0.0022 无效，已废掉 5+ eval 实验）
- `eval_flat_nli_seed44`（QUEUED）和 `eval_flat_nli_s44`（RUNNING）是重复项——前者是先创建但未正确提交的条目，后者是实际在跑的。下一个 session 不要重复提交 seed44 eval
- C3 claim（CED > flat-NLI）已被 D056 降级——**不要**在汇报中声称 CED 显著优于 flat-NLI
- `eval_plan_014_no_evidence` 标记 PREPARING 但**已经完成**（rel_f1=0.4893），只是 registry 状态没清理。不要重跑

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- **服务器**: rtx5090-4x（4× RTX 5090 32GB），account=root
- **工作目录**: `/root/autodl-tmp/freige`

- **模型**: SFT checkpoint `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/outputs/sft_qwen3_4b/`；各 RSFT checkpoint 在 `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/outputs/rsft_*/`
- **数据**: `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/data/docred/`
- **Eval 输出**: `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/eval_results/` (14 个方法的 predictions.json 已就绪)
- **EDCR 诊断脚本**: `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/edcr_diagnostic.py`（CPU-only，14 方法已 ready）
- **活跃进程**:
  - reeval_flat_nli_s43: PID 266178, tmux, cuda:1, cron 监控中
  - reeval_rsft_topk3: PID 268485, tmux, cuda:2, cron 监控中
  - eval_flat_nli_s44: PID 270462, tmux, cuda:0, cron 监控中
  - eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2: PID 262075, cuda:3, **ANALYZING**（进程已结束，等结果处理）
- **活跃 Worker**: `worker_analysis_flat_nli_vs_ced_comparison_20260515T232837`（分析 flat-NLI vs CED 对比）

### 3. 📊 当前状态
Session #12 的主要工作：处理 D064 bug（eval 缺 --sft_adapter）引发的大规模重跑，完成 plan_009 forgetting dynamics figure，完成 plan_001 seed44/45 训练，确认 bf16 GRPO 无 decoupling (D065/D066)。

**RUNNING (3)**:
- `reeval_flat_nli_s43` (cuda:1) — D064 fix 重跑 flat-NLI s43 eval
- `reeval_rsft_topk3` (cuda:2) — D064 fix 重跑 RSFT top_k=3 eval
- `eval_flat_nli_s44` (cuda:0) — flat-NLI seed44 全量 eval（训练刚完成）

**ANALYZING (1)**:
- `eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2` (cuda:3) — DPO 1ep eval 已完成，**等待结果收割**。这是最高优先级待处理项——DPO 结果决定论文中 DPO 的定位

**等待中**:
- Worker `worker_analysis_flat_nli_vs_ced_comparison` 仍在运行
- 3 个 cron 监控 3 个 RUNNING 实验

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **立即**: `eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2` 已 ANALYZING → spawn Worker 读 log + 提取 metrics → update_registry → 汇报 Director DPO 定位决策 → cuda:3 释放后提交 `plan_003_edcr_diagnostic`
- `reeval_flat_nli_s43` cron 回调 → 提取 metrics → 与 s42(0.4539) 对比 → cuda:1 释放后提交 `eval_flat_nli_seed45`
- `eval_flat_nli_s44` cron 回调 → 提取 metrics → 与 CED mean(0.4596) 对比
- `reeval_rsft_topk3` cron 回调 → 提取 metrics → 与 CED top_k=1(~0.46) 和 SFT(0.4057) 对比
- 三个 flat-NLI seed eval (s43/s44/s45) 全部完成 → 汇总 paired comparison CED vs flat-NLI → 更新 C3 claim 状态
- `plan_003_edcr_diagnostic` 提交（CPU-only，等任意 GPU 释放即可提交，但 EDCR 脚本本身不需 GPU，可以直接 Worker SSH 执行）
- 所有 P0 eval 完成 → plan_002 test set eval（D057 上次失败需诊断）
- CED 补种 s43/s45（paired t-test 需 matched pairs，目前 CED 仅 s42/s44）

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心结论链**:
- D054: GRPO catastrophic forgetting 确认（3 reward × 多 lr）
- D056: CED ≈ flat-NLI 统计不可区分 → C3 降级
- D060: no-evidence ablation (0.4893 vs 0.4106) 量化 evidence tax ~8pp；CED reranker 恢复 97%
- D064: **重大 bug** — 多个 eval 缺 --sft_adapter，rel_f1 全部无效，当前 3 个 RUNNING 是修复后重跑
- D065/D066: bf16 GRPO 无 decoupling → decoupling 是 QLoRA-specific artifact

**最容易判断错的事**:
1. **不要把 eval_flat_nli_seed44 (QUEUED) 当作需要提交的实验**——它是 eval_flat_nli_s44 (RUNNING) 的残留条目
2. **DPO 1ep eval (ANALYZING) 是当前最高优先级**——它决定 DPO 在论文中是 "negative result supplement" 还是 "competitive baseline"。上次 3ep DPO rel_f1=0.3429（过拟合），1ep 如果好则重新定位
3. **plan_003 EDCR diagnostic 不需要 GPU**——是纯 CPU 计算，可以直接 SSH Worker 执行，不必等 GPU 释放
4. **cuda:3 马上就能空出来**（DPO eval 已 ANALYZING）→ 优先提交 plan_003_edcr_diagnostic 或开始 CED 补种训练

      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"

{
  "parentUuid": "45503c6a-dd8b-46f7-841d-b78b94fc47cf",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

I'm deciding between submit_training_job and Worker SSH execution for the EDCR diagnostic — since it's CPU-only and short-running, a direct SSH execution through a Worker makes more sense than the overhead of job submission. So I'll spawn three parallel actions: DPO log analysis via SSH, the eval job through submit_training_job, and EDCR diagnostic execution via Worker. For the EDCR script, I need to reference the prediction files in `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/eval_results/` and run the diagnostic script from `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/scripts/edcr_diagnostic.py`.

I should also update eval_plan_014_no_evidence in the registry with its final rel_f1 score, though that's a significant state transition from PREPARING to DONE that I'll handle separately. Before reporting to the Director, I need to review the resource-saturation skill as well.

One issue though — eval_flat_nli_seed45 is supposed to use cuda:0 but that GPU is currently occupied.  Since cuda:3 is available and the submit_training_job command handles GPU assignment through CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES rather than hardcoding it, I can run it there and just update the registry spec to reflect cuda:3 instead.

Now I'm ready to execute the three parallel actions: spawning the DPO Worker to analyze the logs from rtx5090-4x, submitting eval_flat_nli_seed45 to cuda:3, and spawning the EDCR diagnostic Worker.

I have two options: wait for cuda:3 to be freed from plan_003_edcr_diagnostic, or deallocate the GPU from that job since it's CPU-only and will run via SSH through the EDCR Worker instead. Looking at the roadmap, cuda:3 is currently running eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2, which should complete before plan_003_edcr_diagnostic starts, so I need to understand the actual GPU allocation timeline.

Since plan_003_edcr_diagnostic doesn't actually need GPU resources when executed via SSH, I should update the registry to remove cuda:3 from its allocation rather than waiting for the DPO eval to finish. Let me verify what the current spec shows.
